## PLATFORM HARDENING ROADMAP — Phase 1 "Make It Real"

> Converts the library/contract foundation (EVT/RISK/PRED/DATA epics) into a running, persistent, secured, observable system. New module prefixes: `ORCH` (runtime), `PERS` (persistence), `SEC` (security), `CICD` (delivery), `OBS` (telemetry). Sequenced by ROI ranks 1–6. Subtasks scoped 1–3 days; each epic carries its own `Tests:` subtask(s) per board convention.

### Platform Runtime — risk-engine service + durable state

_Objective: deploy a concrete `risk/api/v1.Engine` that ingests state, recomputes, publishes, and survives restarts. Unblocks the entire risk capability (ROI #1–2)._

_(epic complete — ORCH-01a..f + PERS-01a..e in DONE)_

### Security & Identity

_Objective: zero-trust transport + workload identity + secrets management. Gate to any institutional client or auditor (ROI #3)._

_(epic complete — SEC-01a..e in DONE)_

### Delivery, Build & Observability

_Objective: a CI/CD safety net for the `kanz` module, published schema SDKs, and the telemetry the DATA-08/09 dashboards already assume (ROI #4–6)._

- [ ] **DEBT-01a** Run `schema-release.yml` → publish + tag the `kanz-schemas-go` companion module repo · _(kanz-schemas CI)_
- [ ] **DEBT-01b** Publish Python (PyPI/private index) + TS (npm `@kanz-eng/kanz-schemas`) SDK artifacts · _(kanz-schemas CI)_
- [ ] **DEBT-01c** Pin published SDK versions; remove the local `replace github.com/kanz-eng/kanz-schemas-go => ../kanz-schemas/gen/go` in `kanz/go.mod` (+ kanz-py/ts equivalents) · `kanz/go.mod`, `kanz-py/pyproject.toml`
- [ ] **DEBT-01d** CI guard: fail the build if any `replace` directive or stale gen/ path reappears · `.github/workflows/kanz-ci.yml`

> **DEBT-01a..d are blocked on external publishing** (PyPI/npm/companion-repo tags) not doable on the dev box, and the `replace` directive DEBT-01d guards against is still load-bearing until DEBT-01a..c land (EVT-17b). Deferred until a release pipeline run.

_(OBS-01a..e epic complete — see DONE)_

## PLATFORM HARDENING ROADMAP — Phase 5 "Aladdin Parity — Front-to-Back"

> Turns the risk/analytics core into a whole-platform investment system: a governed trading surface, a mandate/compliance engine, real pricing depth (derivatives + fixed income), and the portfolio-manager workflow (attribution, construction, liquidity). New prefixes: `OMS` (order/execution), `COMP` (compliance/mandate), `DERIV` (derivatives pricing), `FI` (fixed-income analytics), `PERF` (performance attribution), `OPT` (portfolio construction), `LIQ` (liquidity risk). Trading introduces the platform's first first-class COMMAND flow (the `command.v1` framework was built for exactly this, EVT-19); everything stays event-driven and query-read-only per the RISK-02 boundary. Sequenced ROI #24–#30 — OMS is the spine the rest trade through, COMP gates it, and the pricing/PM epics deepen the analytics the engine already registers via the RISK-07 `MeasureFunc` seam.

### Order & Execution Management (OMS/EMS)

_Objective: a governed, command-driven order lifecycle — submit/amend/cancel → route → fill — feeding positions back through the event spine so risk recomputes on in-flight trades. The platform's first write path and the front-office foundation (ROI #24)._

- [x] **OMS-01a** Order/execution schemas: `order.v1` — `SubmitOrder`/`AmendOrder`/`CancelOrder` commands (embed `command.v1.CommandMetadata`, EVT-19) + the `Order` aggregate lifecycle FACTs (`OrderAccepted`/`Rejected`/`Routed`/`PartiallyFilled`/`Filled`/`Cancelled`/`Expired`) + `Fill`/`Execution` · `kanz-schemas/proto/order/v1/` — _(see IN PROGRESS)_
- [x] **OMS-01b** `oms` service: validate→accept→emit lifecycle FACTs over an order-state aggregate, durable + replayable via the PERS-01 snapshot/log pattern; idempotent command handling on the bus dedup (EVT-17d) · `kanz/services/oms/` — _(see IN PROGRESS)_
- [x] **OMS-01c** Execution layer (EMS): smart-order-router + a `Venue` seam (log/sim venue that matches & fills by default; a real FIX/venue adapter is composition-root, the DEBT-02 inject-the-side-effect stance) emitting `Fill` FACTs · `kanz/services/oms/internal/execution/` — _(see IN PROGRESS)_
- [x] **OMS-01d** Governed write surface at the gateway: `POST /v1/orders` → command publish, AUTH-01 `Principal` bound to `CommandMetadata.issuer` (AUTH-01c forged-issuer guard), idempotency-key dedup (API-01d), per-tenant · `kanz/services/api-gateway/internal/` — _(see IN PROGRESS)_
- [x] **OMS-01e** Position integration: `Fill` FACTs update `domain.v1.PositionState` on the event-driven path so the risk-engine recompute (ORCH-01) + exposures reflect in-flight trades; realized/unrealized PnL from fills · `kanz/internal/risk/ingest/` — _(see IN PROGRESS)_
- [x] **OMS-01f** Pre-trade gate hook: order accept calls the COMP-01 check (deny-by-default on breach) before emitting `OrderAccepted` — the OMS↔COMP seam · `kanz/services/oms/internal/` — _(see IN PROGRESS)_
- [x] **OMS-01g** Tests: order state-machine transitions, idempotent re-submit (one order per idempotency-key), fill→position propagation, issuer-mismatch rejection, compliance-gate reject path · `kanz/services/oms/internal/` — _(see IN PROGRESS; **closes the OMS-01 epic**)_

### Compliance & Mandate Engine

_Objective: pre- and post-trade enforcement of portfolio mandates — concentration, exclusion, leverage, restriction lists — deny-by-default, every decision audited. Aladdin's signature differentiator (ROI #25)._

- [x] **COMP-01a** Mandate/rule schemas: `compliance.v1` — `Mandate` (tenant/portfolio-scoped ruleset), rule types (concentration %, asset-class/sector restriction, issuer exclusion list, gross-leverage cap, currency restriction), `ComplianceResult` (PASS/WARN/BREACH + violated rule + evidence) · `kanz-schemas/proto/compliance/v1/`
- [x] **COMP-01b** Rule-evaluation engine: a pure `RuleFunc` registry mirroring the RISK-07 `MeasureFunc` registry; evaluates a candidate portfolio against a mandate; deny-by-default on an unknown rule · `kanz/internal/compliance/`
- [x] **COMP-01c** Pre-trade gate (consumed by OMS-01f): project the post-trade book (current + order delta), reuse `compute.ComputeExposure` for the hypothetical exposures, run the rules, reject on BREACH · `kanz/internal/compliance/gate.go`
- [x] **COMP-01d** Post-trade monitor: consume position/exposure FACTs, continuously re-evaluate mandates, emit a `ComplianceBreach` FACT (FACT-grade, never shed) on a market-move-induced passive breach; feeds AUTO-01 for halts · `kanz/services/compliance/internal/monitor/`
- [x] **COMP-01e** Decision logging: every compliance decision (pass/warn/breach) into the observation stream via the AUTH-01d `Audited`-recorder pattern → AUDIT-01 hash-chain · `kanz/services/compliance/internal/audit.go`
- [x] **COMP-01f** Mandate lifecycle: tenant-scoped mandate changes as `lifecycle.v1.ConfigChanged` FACTs, point-in-time correct (which mandate applied when) · `kanz/services/compliance/`
- [x] **COMP-01g** Tests: rule matrix (concentration/exclusion/leverage allow+breach), pre-trade reject blocks the order, passive post-trade breach emits + escalates, deny-by-default unknown rule, point-in-time mandate resolution · `kanz/internal/compliance/`

### Derivatives Pricing & Greeks

_Objective: replace the RISK-07 placeholder `Delta` with real per-instrument pricing and full Greeks, registered through the same `MeasureFunc` seam. Turns the `DERIVATIVE` asset class from a tag into analytics (ROI #26)._

- [x] **DERIV-01a** Contract-terms schemas (additive to `reference.v1`): `OptionTerms` (underlying, strike, expiry, call/put, exercise style), `SwapTerms` (legs, tenor, fixed/float), `FutureTerms` · `kanz-schemas/proto/reference/v1/contract.proto`
- [x] **DERIV-01b** Pricing library: Black-Scholes + binomial (American) option pricers discounting off the FI-01 curve; analytic + bumped Greeks (Δ/Γ/Vega/Θ/ρ); pure, deterministic, `Decimal`-safe · `kanz/internal/risk/pricing/`
- [x] **DERIV-01c** Implied-vol surface (strike×expiry) built from market option quotes (`market.v1`), point-in-time via the MODEL-01b bitemporal store; interpolation · `kanz/internal/risk/pricing/volsurface/`
- [x] **DERIV-01d** Real Greeks measures: register per-position + aggregate portfolio Greeks via `compute.Register`, replacing the placeholder `Delta`; lands the MODEL-01 engine-registration seam · `kanz/internal/risk/compute/greeks.go`
- [x] **DERIV-01e** Full-revaluation scenarios: reprice options under price/vol shocks in the scenario library (extends MODEL-01h); a delta-gamma VaR variant · `kanz/internal/risk/scenario/`
- [x] **DERIV-01f** Tests: BS vs reference values, put-call parity, Greeks vs finite-difference, American ≥ European, vol-surface round-trip, portfolio-Greek aggregation · `kanz/internal/risk/pricing/`

### Fixed-Income Analytics

_Objective: yield-curve construction and bond risk (duration, convexity, DV01, key-rate, OAS) so the `FIXED_INCOME` asset class carries real measures and curve scenarios. Completes the MODEL-01 curve-shift deferral (ROI #27)._

- [x] **FI-01a** Bond/curve schemas (additive to `reference.v1`): `BondTerms` (coupon, frequency, maturity, day-count, face) + `YieldCurve` (tenor→rate, curve type) · `kanz-schemas/proto/reference/v1/`
- [x] **FI-01b** Curve construction: bootstrap a zero/discount curve from par/swap quotes, point-in-time via the store, linear/log-linear interpolation · `kanz/internal/risk/pricing/curve/`
- [x] **FI-01c** Bond analytics: price/yield, accrued interest (day-count conventions), Macaulay/modified duration, convexity, DV01, key-rate durations · `kanz/internal/risk/pricing/bond.go`
- [x] **FI-01d** FI risk measures: register duration/DV01/convexity/spread-duration as `MeasureFunc`s; portfolio interest-rate sensitivity · `kanz/internal/risk/compute/`
- [x] **FI-01e** Curve scenarios: parallel / steepener / butterfly yield-curve shifts in the scenario library (lands the MODEL-01 curve-shift carried-forward), repricing bonds · `kanz/internal/risk/scenario/library/`
- [x] **FI-01f** Tests: known-bond price/yield/duration vectors, day-count correctness, DV01 vs bump, curve-bootstrap round-trip, curve-shift repricing · `kanz/internal/risk/pricing/`

### Performance Measurement & Attribution

_Objective: time-/money-weighted returns, benchmark-relative active return, and Brinson attribution — point-in-time correct over the bitemporal history. The "how did we do and why" the LAKE-01 plane was built to support (ROI #28)._

- [x] **PERF-01a** Performance schemas: `performance.v1` — `ReturnPeriod` (TWR/MWR), `BenchmarkDefinition`, `AttributionResult` (allocation/selection/interaction) · `kanz-schemas/proto/performance/v1/`
- [x] **PERF-01b** Return calculation: time- and money-weighted returns over the bitemporal position/price history (point-in-time, reuse the store `AsOf`), flow-aware · `kanz/internal/performance/`
- [x] **PERF-01c** Benchmark plane: benchmark constituents + returns as reference/market data; portfolio-vs-benchmark active return · `kanz/services/performance/`
- [x] **PERF-01d** Brinson attribution: allocation/selection/interaction by sector (reuse `factor.Classifier` bucketing), multi-period linking (Carino) · `kanz/internal/performance/attribution.go`
- [x] **PERF-01e** Ex-post risk: tracking error, information ratio, Sharpe, benchmark beta from the return series · `kanz/internal/performance/`
- [x] **PERF-01f** Tests: TWR vs known flows, Brinson effects sum to active return, multi-period linking, point-in-time no-future-leakage · `kanz/internal/performance/`

### Portfolio Construction & Optimization

_Objective: mean-variance / risk-parity construction and rebalancing under the same mandate constraints COMP-01 enforces, emitting proposed orders into OMS-01. The portfolio-manager's forward workflow (ROI #29)._

- [x] **OPT-01a** Optimization schemas: `optimization.v1` — `ObjectiveSpec` (max-return / min-variance / max-Sharpe / risk-parity), `ConstraintSet` (weight bounds, sector/asset caps, turnover, long-only), `RebalanceProposal` (target weights + trade list) · `kanz-schemas/proto/optimization/v1/`
- [x] **OPT-01b** Optimizer: mean-variance (QP) over the MODEL-01e covariance + expected returns, a risk-parity option, deterministic; small in-module solver behind a `Solver` seam (no heavy dep) · `kanz/internal/optimization/`
- [x] **OPT-01c** Constraint engine: reuse the COMP-01b mandate rules as optimization constraints (one source of truth — a book you can't hold, you can't optimize into) · `kanz/internal/optimization/constraints.go`
- [x] **OPT-01d** Rebalancing: diff target vs current → a minimal, turnover/cost-aware trade list; emits a `RebalanceProposal` (proposed, human-in-the-loop per the AUTO-01 conservative stance) · `kanz/internal/optimization/rebalance.go`
- [x] **OPT-01e** Proposal→OMS bridge: an approved proposal materializes OMS-01 `SubmitOrder` commands (issuer-bound, pre-trade compliance re-checked) · `kanz/services/optimization/`
- [x] **OPT-01f** Tests: known mean-variance frontier point, constraints bind (caps respected), risk-parity equal risk contribution, rebalance trade-list minimality, proposal→order mapping · `kanz/internal/optimization/`

### Liquidity Risk

_Objective: liquidation-horizon, market-impact, and liquidity-adjusted VaR — the liquidity dimension the quote feed already carries but nothing models (ROI #30)._

- [x] **LIQ-01a** Liquidity-data schemas: average daily volume (ADV) + bid-ask spread / depth fields (additive to `reference.v1`/`market.v1`) · `kanz-schemas/proto/` — _(see IN PROGRESS)_
- [x] **LIQ-01b** Liquidation-horizon model: days-to-liquidate per position (size ÷ participation-rate × ADV) → portfolio liquidation profile · `kanz/internal/risk/liquidity/` — _(see IN PROGRESS)_
- [x] **LIQ-01c** Market-impact / liquidity-at-risk: Almgren-Chriss-style impact cost; liquidity-adjusted VaR (widen by liquidation-horizon spread cost) · `kanz/internal/risk/liquidity/` — _(see IN PROGRESS)_
- [x] **LIQ-01d** Liquidity measures + stress: register liquidation-horizon/LVaR as `MeasureFunc`s; a liquidity-stress scenario (spreads widen, ADV drops) in the scenario library · `kanz/internal/risk/compute/` — _(see IN PROGRESS)_
- [x] **LIQ-01e** Tests: horizon monotonic in size, LVaR ≥ VaR, stress widens the horizon, ADV-zero handling · `kanz/internal/risk/liquidity/` — _(see IN PROGRESS; **closes the LIQ-01 epic**)_

## PLATFORM HARDENING ROADMAP — Phase 6 "Institutional Risk & Capital Depth"

> The quant/risk/capital machinery that *is* the BlackRock product: a multi-factor risk model, counterparty/XVA, collateral & margin, structured-product cashflow models, regulatory capital & stress, an accounting book-of-record, and post-trade settlement. New prefixes: `FACTOR` (multi-factor risk model), `XVA` (counterparty/valuation adjustments), `COLL` (collateral/margin/financing), `STRUCT` (securitized & structured products), `REG` (regulatory capital & stress), `IBOR` (book-of-record accounting + corporate actions), `POST` (post-trade lifecycle). Builds on Phase 5: priced instruments (DERIV/FI) feed the factor model + XVA; OMS fills feed settlement + the book-of-record. ROI #31–#37.

### Multi-Factor Risk Model (Aladdin-class)

_Objective: a fundamental + statistical multi-factor risk model — factor exposures, a factor-covariance matrix, factor returns, and specific (idiosyncratic) risk — so VaR, ex-ante tracking error, and risk attribution decompose by factor, not just by name. The analytical core BlackRock is built on (ROI #31)._

- [x] **FACTOR-01a** Factor schemas: `factor.v1` — `FactorModel` (style/industry/country/macro factors), `FactorExposure` (instrument→loadings), `FactorReturn` series, `FactorCovariance` matrix · `kanz-schemas/proto/factor/v1/` — _(see IN PROGRESS)_
- [x] **FACTOR-01b** Fundamental factor model: estimate instrument loadings (style: value/momentum/size/quality/vol; industry; country) from reference + market data; cross-sectional regression for factor returns · `kanz/internal/risk/factormodel/` — _(see IN PROGRESS)_
- [x] **FACTOR-01c** Statistical factor model (PCA on the MODEL-01e return covariance) as the model-agnostic complement; blend/select per `FactorModel` config · `kanz/internal/risk/factormodel/statistical.go` — _(see IN PROGRESS)_
- [x] **FACTOR-01d** Factor risk decomposition: portfolio factor exposures, factor-vs-specific risk split, ex-ante tracking error vs a benchmark (reuse PERF-01c benchmarks) · `kanz/internal/risk/factormodel/decompose.go` — _(see IN PROGRESS)_
- [x] **FACTOR-01e** Factor-based measures + VaR: register factor-VaR, marginal/component risk contributions as `MeasureFunc`s; factor-shock scenarios in the library · `kanz/internal/risk/compute/`, `scenario/` — _(see IN PROGRESS)_
- [x] **FACTOR-01f** Tests: loadings reproduce known exposures, factor+specific = total risk, component contributions sum to portfolio risk, factor-VaR vs MODEL-01 historical-VaR reconciliation · `kanz/internal/risk/factormodel/` — _(see IN PROGRESS; **closes the FACTOR-01 epic**)_

### Counterparty Credit Risk & XVA

_Objective: counterparty exposure (PFE/EPE), credit valuation adjustments (CVA/DVA/FVA), and SA-CCR exposure — the derivatives-book risk Phase-5 pricing makes computable (ROI #32)._

- [x] **XVA-01a** Counterparty/netting schemas: `xva.v1` — `Counterparty`, `NettingSet`, `CSA` (collateral terms), `CreditCurve` (CDS-implied hazard rates) · `kanz-schemas/proto/xva/v1/` — _(see IN PROGRESS)_
- [x] **XVA-01b** Exposure simulation: Monte-Carlo future-exposure paths over netting sets (reuse the MODEL-01e correlated-scenario engine + DERIV/FI pricers); EPE/PFE profiles · `kanz/internal/risk/xva/` — _(see IN PROGRESS)_
- [x] **XVA-01c** Valuation adjustments: CVA/DVA/FVA from exposure profiles × credit curves × funding spreads · `kanz/internal/risk/xva/adjustments.go` — _(see IN PROGRESS)_
- [x] **XVA-01d** SA-CCR regulatory exposure (add-on by asset class, the Basel standardized approach) feeding REG-01 capital · `kanz/internal/risk/xva/saccr.go` — _(see IN PROGRESS)_
- [x] **XVA-01e** XVA measures + scenarios: register CVA/PFE as `MeasureFunc`s; counterparty-default + credit-spread-widening scenarios · `kanz/internal/risk/compute/`, `scenario/` — _(see IN PROGRESS)_
- [x] **XVA-01f** Tests: PFE monotone in horizon/vol, netting reduces exposure, CVA vs an analytic approximation, SA-CCR vs Basel worked examples · `kanz/internal/risk/xva/` — _(see IN PROGRESS; **closes the XVA-01 epic**)_

### Collateral, Margin & Financing

_Objective: initial/variation margin (incl. ISDA SIMM), collateral optimization, repo & securities-lending financing — the cash/collateral plane that turns positions into funded positions (ROI #33)._

- [x] **COLL-01a** Collateral schemas: `collateral.v1` — `MarginCall`, `CollateralAgreement`, `EligibilitySchedule` (haircuts), `FinancingTrade` (repo/sec-lending) · `kanz-schemas/proto/collateral/v1/` — _(see IN PROGRESS)_
- [x] **COLL-01b** Margin engine: variation margin from MtM; initial margin via ISDA SIMM (sensitivity-based) reusing DERIV-01 Greeks · `kanz/internal/collateral/` — _(see IN PROGRESS)_
- [x] **COLL-01c** Collateral optimization: cheapest-to-deliver allocation across agreements under eligibility/haircut constraints (an OPT-01b-style allocation) · `kanz/internal/collateral/optimize.go` — _(see IN PROGRESS)_
- [x] **COLL-01d** Financing & cash projection: repo/sec-lending economics + a forward cash-ladder (settlement-date cash, extends the Portfolio `cashBalance`) · `kanz/internal/collateral/financing.go` — _(see IN PROGRESS)_
- [x] **COLL-01e** Tests: VM tracks MtM, SIMM vs ISDA unit tests, optimization respects eligibility + minimizes cost, cash-ladder nets to balance · `kanz/internal/collateral/` — _(see IN PROGRESS; **closes the COLL-01 epic**)_

### Securitized & Structured Products

_Objective: cashflow + prepayment models for MBS/ABS/CLO and amortizing structures, so structured products carry real price/risk instead of an asset-class tag (ROI #34)._

- [ ] **STRUCT-01a** Structured-product schemas: `MBSTerms`/`ABSTerms`/`Tranche` (waterfall, attachment/detachment) + prepayment-assumption inputs (additive to `reference.v1`) · `kanz-schemas/proto/reference/v1/structured.proto`
- [ ] **STRUCT-01b** Cashflow engine: deterministic waterfall projection (principal/interest, tranching, triggers) over a scheduled amortization · `kanz/internal/risk/pricing/structured/`
- [ ] **STRUCT-01c** Prepayment/default model: CPR/CDR (SMM) + a behavioral prepay curve driven by the FI-01 rate curve · `kanz/internal/risk/pricing/structured/prepay.go`
- [ ] **STRUCT-01d** OAS & risk: option-adjusted spread, effective duration/convexity under rate paths (reuse FI-01 curve scenarios); register as `MeasureFunc`s · `kanz/internal/risk/compute/`
- [ ] **STRUCT-01e** Tests: waterfall conserves cash, faster prepay shortens WAL, OAS round-trips to price, effective-duration sign/monotonicity · `kanz/internal/risk/pricing/structured/`

### Regulatory Capital & Stress

_Objective: regulatory capital and supervisory stress — FRTB market-risk capital, SA-CCR (XVA-01d), and CCAR/DFAST/Form-PF-style firmwide stress over the scenario engine (ROI #35)._

- [ ] **REG-01a** Regulatory schemas: `regulatory.v1` — `CapitalCharge` (per framework), `RegulatoryScenario`, `RegReport` (FRTB/Form PF/AIFMD line items) · `kanz-schemas/proto/regulatory/v1/`
- [ ] **REG-01b** FRTB market-risk capital: the sensitivities-based method (delta/vega/curvature buckets + correlations) reusing DERIV/FI sensitivities · `kanz/internal/regulatory/frtb/`
- [ ] **REG-01c** Firmwide stress framework: CCAR/DFAST-style macro scenarios expanded to instrument shocks (extends MODEL-01h library) + reverse stress testing (find the scenario that breaches) · `kanz/internal/regulatory/stress/`
- [ ] **REG-01d** Regulatory reporting: template-driven report generation through the AUDIT-01 reporting plane (FRTB, Form PF, AIFMD), point-in-time + signed · `kanz/services/audit/` (reuse), `kanz/internal/regulatory/report.go`
- [ ] **REG-01e** Tests: FRTB worked-example reconciliation, reverse-stress finds a known breach scenario, report line-item completeness · `kanz/internal/regulatory/`

### Accounting Book-of-Record & Corporate Actions (IBOR/ABOR)

_Objective: an investment + accounting book-of-record — positions, cash, accruals, NAV — kept correct through corporate actions and reconciled to custodians. The system-of-record beneath every number (ROI #36)._

- [ ] **IBOR-01a** Book-of-record schemas: `accounting.v1` — `LedgerEntry`, `CorporateAction` (split/dividend/merger/coupon), `NAV`, `Accrual` · `kanz-schemas/proto/accounting/v1/`
- [ ] **IBOR-01b** Position/cash ledger: an event-sourced IBOR folding OMS-01 fills + cash movements (reuse the PERS-01 snapshot/replay pattern) into a point-in-time book · `kanz/services/accounting/`
- [ ] **IBOR-01c** Corporate-action processing: apply splits/dividends/mergers/coupons as events that adjust positions/cash + restate history (bitemporal, knowledge-time correct) · `kanz/services/accounting/internal/corpact/`
- [ ] **IBOR-01d** NAV + accruals: daily NAV, P&L attribution to cash/price/FX/corporate-actions, interest/dividend accruals · `kanz/services/accounting/internal/nav.go`
- [ ] **IBOR-01e** Custodian reconciliation: reconcile the IBOR against custodian/administrator statements (reuse the DATA-05 reconciler), break detection · `kanz/internal/integrity/` (reuse), `kanz/services/accounting/`
- [ ] **IBOR-01f** Tests: ledger replays to the same NAV, a stock split conserves market value, corporate-action restatement is knowledge-time correct, custodian-break detection · `kanz/services/accounting/`

### Post-Trade Lifecycle

_Objective: the trade lifecycle after the fill — confirmation/affirmation, settlement, and custodian status — closing the loop OMS-01 opens (ROI #37)._

- [ ] **POST-01a** Post-trade schemas: `settlement.v1` — `TradeConfirmation`, `SettlementInstruction`, `SettlementStatus` (matched/affirmed/settled/failed) · `kanz-schemas/proto/settlement/v1/`
- [ ] **POST-01b** Confirm/affirm: match OMS-01 fills to counterparty confirmations, affirm or flag breaks · `kanz/services/oms/internal/posttrade/`
- [ ] **POST-01c** Settlement: generate settlement instructions, track T+N status via a `SettlementVenue` seam (log/sim default; SWIFT/DTCC adapter is composition-root) · `kanz/services/oms/internal/posttrade/settle.go`
- [ ] **POST-01d** Fails management: detect/age settlement fails, emit a FACT feeding AUTO-01 + the IBOR-01e reconciliation · `kanz/services/oms/internal/posttrade/`
- [ ] **POST-01e** Tests: confirm-match break detection, settlement state machine, fail aging + escalation · `kanz/services/oms/internal/posttrade/`

## PLATFORM HARDENING ROADMAP — Phase 7 "Whole-Enterprise & Frontier Bets"

> Extends beyond public-markets trading to the full BlackRock enterprise surface: private markets (eFront), climate & sustainability, wealth/advisory, a golden-source data master, and a Claude-powered analytics copilot. New prefixes: `ALT` (private markets/alternatives), `CLIMATE` (sustainability analytics), `WEALTH` (advisory platform), `MASTER` (golden-source data), `COPILOT` (AI analytics layer). ROI #38–#42.

### Private Markets & Alternatives (eFront-class)

_Objective: private equity / credit / real-assets — capital calls, distributions, J-curve, and illiquid valuation — the alternative-assets sleeve a whole-portfolio view requires (ROI #38)._

## IN PROGRESS

**Phase-6 COLL-01a–e DONE — COLL-01 epic COMPLETE** (collateral, margin & financing — the cash/collateral plane that turns positions into FUNDED positions, ROI #33; the downstream of the XVA-01 CSA). New top-level **`kanz/internal/collateral/`** (OUTSIDE `internal/risk`) + `kanz-schemas/proto/collateral/v1/`. **Outside the risk module ⇒ SIMM sensitivities are an INPUT, not a reach-in (RISK-02, the latest application of the rule)**: the arch test forbids `internal/collateral` from importing the DERIV-01 Greek pricers / compute / domain — which is exactly how ISDA SIMM is meant to work (it is a SENSITIVITY-based margin, the CRIF input), so the engine takes delta/vega sensitivities as data and a deployment feeds Greeks computed via the risk engine in. The arch test confirms collateral introduces **no** boundary edge (the 3 failures it reports are pre-existing — `lake/dataset`, `oms/position`, `contract/tenancy`). **COLL-01a** (`collateral.proto`, CODEOWNERS `@kanz-eng/risk-engine @kanz-eng/execution`): `MarginType`, `EligibilityScheduleItem` (asset + eligible + `haircut`), `CollateralAgreement` (threshold/MTA/IA/rounding + the eligibility schedule), `MarginCall` (signed `call_amount` + required/held), `FinancingKind`, `FinancingTrade` (repo/reverse-repo/sec-lending/borrow — principal/rate/haircut/dates) — **money ⇒ Decimal, haircuts/rates ⇒ double** per EVT-14. SDK generated-not-committed (EVT-15a — the internal pkg carries its own float working shapes, kanz build needs no regen). **COLL-01b** (`margin.go` + `simm.go`): `VariationMargin(mtm, held, CSATerms)` — required = max(mtm−threshold,0)+IA, call = gap suppressed below the MTA and **rounded never against the holder** (calls to us round up, calls we owe round down); **VM tracks MtM one-for-one above the threshold**. `SIMM(sensitivities, params)` is the ISDA SIMM **delta-margin aggregation**: `WSₖ=RW·sₖ`, bucket margin `K_b=√(ΣWS²+Σρ·WSₖWSₗ)`, capped bucket sum `S_b=clamp(ΣWS,−K_b,K_b)`, across-bucket `IM=√(ΣK_b²+Σγ·S_bS_c)` — generic in the supervisory RW/ρ/γ so it reproduces the published unit numbers; vega/curvature/base-corr compose on the same shape. **COLL-01c** (`optimize.go`): cheapest-to-deliver allocation — every (agreement, asset) option ranked by **effective cost = Cost/(1−haircut)** and filled cheapest-first under per-asset availability + per-requirement need; an OPT-01b-style allocation but a LINEAR transportation problem with separable per-asset cost, so the greedy is **cost-optimal for the fractional case** (no QP — the "small, dependency-free" stance). Respects eligibility (ineligible assets skipped), grosses up for haircuts (post 400 at a 20% haircut ⇒ use 500), reports `ok=false` on an underfunded pool. **COLL-01d** (`financing.go`): `FinancingTrade.Interest` (ACT/365) + `CashFlows` (repo borrows cash now / repays principal+interest at unwind; reverse-repo the mirror; sec-lending the fee leg), and `BuildLadder(opening, flows)` rolls dated flows into a forward settlement-date **cash ladder** (the forward view of the Portfolio `cashBalance`) — **the ladder nets to opening+Σflows**, and a repo's principal legs cancel so its net cash is exactly −interest (the financing cost). **COLL-01e** (`*_test.go`): VM tracks-MtM + MTA-suppression + rounding-both-ways, **SIMM single-bucket (√300) and two-bucket (√652) worked examples** + netting-reduces-margin, optimization cheapest-to-deliver + eligibility-fallback + haircut-grossup + insufficient-pool, repo interest/flows + **cash-ladder-nets-to-balance** + repo-net-cost-is-interest + date-ordering. **Verified**: `go build ./...`, `go vet ./internal/collateral/...`, `gofmt` clean; `go test ./internal/collateral/...` green; arch test = only the 3 pre-existing failures (collateral adds none). No `-race` locally (no cgo on the dev box); CI runs it. **Carried-forward** (seams delivered): the live SIMM CRIF fed from the DERIV-01 Greeks at the composition root (the sensitivity input shape + the aggregation today); the full SIMM vega/curvature/base-correlation add-ons + the per-tenor IR risk weights/correlation matrices (the delta-margin core + the generic aggregation are delivered); a multi-currency cash ladder with FX (single-currency per rung today); and the margin-call → OMS-01 collateral-movement settlement instruction (the `MarginCall`/`Allocation` shapes are the inputs). **Next in Phase-6**: STRUCT-01 (securitized & structured products), then REG-01 (which the COLL-01b SIMM + XVA-01d SA-CCR feed), IBOR, POST.

**Phase-6 XVA-01a–f DONE — XVA-01 epic COMPLETE** (counterparty credit risk & valuation adjustments — the derivatives-book risk Phase-5 pricing makes computable, ROI #32). New `kanz/internal/risk/xva/` + `compute/xva.go` + `scenario/library/credit.go`, all INSIDE `internal/risk` (no RISK-02 edge — arch test still shows only the 3 pre-existing failures). **XVA-01a** (`kanz-schemas/proto/xva/v1/xva.proto`, CODEOWNERS `@kanz-eng/risk-engine @kanz-eng/quant-ml`): `Counterparty` (+is_index), `CSA` (threshold/MTA/independent-amount, **money ⇒ Decimal** per EVT-14), `NettingSet` (counterparty + trade_ids + CSA), `CreditCurve` (CDS-implied `HazardPoint`s + recovery — **hazards/recovery ⇒ double**), `as_of`-keyed point-in-time. SDK generated-not-committed (EVT-15a — the internal pkg carries its own float working shapes, kanz build needs no regen). **XVA-01b** (`simulate.go`+`util.go`+`pricers.go`): the exposure Monte-Carlo — it **extends the MODEL-01e correlated-scenario idea from one-period P&L to a multi-step exposure PATH**: diffuses the risk factors (`Lognormal` GBM / `Normal` arithmetic-Brownian) along correlated paths (own dependency-free Cholesky), reprices the netting set at each grid date through a **`Pricer` seam that takes time t** (so an option's residual maturity decays along the path — `LinearTrade` and `OptionTrade` reusing `pricing.BlackScholesPrice` are the DERIV-01 reuse), nets, applies the CSA, and reduces each date's distribution to the `ExposureProfile` (EE / PFE-quantile / NEE, plus EPE/EEPE/PeakPFE). The CSA collateralizes exposure above the threshold once it clears the MTA. **XVA-01c** (`adjustments.go`+`credit.go`): `CreditCurve.Survival(t)=exp(−∫h)` (piecewise hazard, `FromCDS` via the credit-triangle h≈spread/(1−R)); `Adjustments` computes **CVA = LGD·Σ EE(tₖ)·DF·[Q(tₖ₋₁)−Q(tₖ)]**, the symmetric **DVA** (NEE × own curve), **FVA** (funding-spread × EE × survival), and **BCVA = CVA−DVA** — the discounted-expected-loss quadrature over the profile. **XVA-01d** (`saccr.go`): a faithful unmargined **Basel SA-CCR** (CRE52) — `EAD = 1.4·(RC + multiplier·ΣAddOn)`, per-trade effective notional `D = δ·d·MF` (supervisory delta incl. the `OptionDelta` Φ-formula, maturity factor √min(M,1), `SupervisoryDuration` for IR), hedging-set aggregation (simple sum for IR/FX; **single-factor-correlation `√((Σρ·AddOnₕ)²+Σ(1−ρ²)AddOnₕ²)`** for equity/credit/commodity), the CRE52 multiplier floor 0.05 — feeds REG-01 capital. **XVA-01e** (`compute/xva.go`+`xva/stress.go`+`scenario/library/credit.go`): `RegisterXVA(ctx,r,XVAProvider)` registers **CVA** (Σ across counterparties) and **PFE** (peak across counterparties) — the provider supplies PRECOMPUTED per-counterparty `xva.Adjustments` (the expensive sim runs upstream/nightly; the measure does only the cheap quadrature/reduction, unlike the per-position FI/Greek reprices). A **credit stress is a new scenario axis** (the liquidity/factor transform pattern, fifth axis): `CreditStress{HazardMult, JumpToDefault}.ApplyTo(adj)` re-marks the counterparty curve (spreads widen ⇒ CVA up; jump-to-default crystallizes the near-term loss), catalog `CREDIT_SPREAD_WIDENING`/`COUNTERPARTY_DEFAULT` joining the GICS/curve/liquidity/factor libraries. **XVA-01f** (`xva/*_test.go`, `compute/xva_test.go`, `scenario/library/credit_test.go`): **PFE monotone in horizon AND in vol** (8k-path seeded sims), **netting reduces exposure** (offsetting trades net to ~0 EPE vs positive standalone), CSA reduces EPE, **CVA matches the closed form LGD·EE·(1−e^{−hT})** (flat profile/hazard/zero-rate telescopes exactly) + discounting lowers it, DVA/FVA/BCVA, **SA-CCR equity worked example EAD=44.8** + the multiplier-below-1 case (V<0 ⇒ 32.869) + within-hedging-set netting + OptionDelta ITM/OTM/short, registered CVA>0 / PFE=peak, credit-stress raises CVA, catalog dispatch. **Verified**: `go build ./...`, `go vet ./internal/risk/...`, `gofmt` clean; `go test ./internal/risk/...` green; arch test = only the 3 pre-existing failures. No `-race` locally (no cgo on the dev box); CI runs it. **Carried-forward** (seams delivered): the concrete `Pricer` adapters from real reference terms + the FI bond/swap pricers (linear/option today), and the live `XVAProvider` fed by a counterparty book + a nightly exposure-sim job at the composition root (static providers + the wire schema today, the same MODEL-01 integration the FI/Greek/factor providers await); wrong-way risk (exposure⟂default-independence assumed today); margined SA-CCR (the MPOR maturity factor) and the full IR maturity-bucket correlation matrix (scoped IR treatment now); American-exposure via the binomial pricer behind the Pricer seam. **Next in Phase-6**: COLL-01 (collateral/margin/financing — the CSA terms XVA-01a defined drive it), then STRUCT/REG/IBOR/POST.

**Phase-6 FACTOR-01a–f DONE — FACTOR-01 epic COMPLETE** (multi-factor risk model — the analytical core an Aladdin-class platform is built on, ROI #31; opens Phase-6 "Institutional Risk & Capital Depth"). A fundamental + statistical factor model so risk decomposes into a FACTOR (systematic) part and a SPECIFIC (idiosyncratic) part: `rᵢ = Σₖ Bᵢₖ·fₖ + εᵢ`. New `kanz/internal/risk/factormodel/` + `compute/factorrisk.go` + `scenario/factorreval.go`, all INSIDE `internal/risk` (no RISK-02 edge — arch test still shows only the 3 pre-existing failures). **FACTOR-01a** (`kanz-schemas/proto/factor/v1/factor.proto`, CODEOWNERS `@kanz-eng/risk-engine @kanz-eng/quant-ml`): `FactorType` (STYLE/INDUSTRY/COUNTRY/MACRO/STATISTICAL), `Factor`, `FactorModel` (model_id+as_of point-in-time, ordered factors), `FactorExposure` (instrument→loadings + specific_variance), `FactorReturn` series, `FactorCovariance` (row-major K×K flat) — every loading/return/covariance is `double` per EVT-14 (a factor model carries no money). SDK generated-not-committed (EVT-15a — like FI/LIQ, the internal pkg carries its own float working shapes, so the kanz build needs no regen). **FACTOR-01b** (`factormodel/fundamental.go` + `linalg.go`): the BARRA-style fundamental model — loadings OBSERVED from `Characteristics` (style scores **z-scored cross-sectionally**, industry/country **0/1 dummies**), factor RETURNS estimated by a **per-period cross-sectional OLS** (ridge-regularized normal equations `(BᵀB+λI)fₜ=Bᵀrₜ` — the ridge keeps collinear industry/country dummies invertible), factor covariance = sample cov of the factor-return series, specific variance = per-instrument residual variance. **Dependency-free linear algebra** (the VaR/curve/optimization "own the small numerical core, no gonum" stance): Gaussian-elimination solver, **cyclic-Jacobi symmetric eigendecomposition** (for the PCA), sample cov/var, z-score, and an **Acklam inverse-normal CDF** for the parametric z. **FACTOR-01c** (`statistical.go`): the PCA model — eigendecompose the return covariance, keep the top-K principal components as latent factors (loadings = eigenvector components, factor cov = diag of eigenvalues, specific var = total − explained). The `Config{Type}` selector dispatches Fundamental / Statistical / **Blend**, where **Blend is the proper hybrid**: fundamental factors PLUS statistical factors fit on the fundamental RESIDUALS (orthogonal by construction ⇒ a **block-diagonal** factor covariance), so it keeps interpretable factors while capturing leftover common structure. **To avoid a `compute`↔`factormodel` cycle, factormodel declares its OWN structural `ReturnsProvider`** (same signature as compute's) — compute's `StoreReturnsProvider` satisfies both, so the engine wires one provider into VaR and the factor model (one consistent history, the reconciliation hinge). **FACTOR-01d** (`decompose.go`): portfolio factor exposures `e=Bᵀv`, the factor-vs-specific split (`Total²=eᵀFe + Σvᵢ²dᵢ`, independent by ε⟂f), per-factor AND per-instrument risk contributions via the **Euler identity** (component `vᵢ(Σv)ᵢ/σ` sums to total; factor `eₖ(Fe)ₖ/σ` + specific sums to total), and **ex-ante tracking error** = total risk of the active position `v_p−v_b` — benchmark weights are an INPUT (the PERF-01c `BenchmarkDefinition` constituents fed in), the "covariance is an input" stance, NOT a reach into the performance module. **FACTOR-01e** (`compute/factorrisk.go` + `scenario/factorreval.go` + `scenario/library/factor.go`): `RegisterFactorRisk(ctx,r,ModelProvider)` registers `FactorVaR99` (= z₀.₉₉·√(vᵀΣv)) / `SystematicRisk` / `SpecificRisk`, each resolving the model at `p.AsOf()` through a **per-snapshot `ModelProvider`** (the universe-wide analog of the FI/Greek per-instrument providers; marginal/component contributions are vectors, delivered via `Decompose`, not the scalar registry). A **factor shock is a new scenario axis**: `EvaluateFactorShock` reprices each position `MVᵢ←MVᵢ(1+Σₖ Bᵢₖ·shockₖ)` — a regime ("momentum crashes 10%, value rallies") no single price/sector/curve shift can express — a separate entry point (zero blast radius), with a `MOMENTUM_CRASH`/`FLIGHT_TO_QUALITY` catalog joining the GICS/curve/liquidity libraries. **FACTOR-01f** (`factormodel/*_test.go`, `compute/factorrisk_test.go`, `compute/var/factor_reconcile_test.go`, `scenario/*_test.go`): loadings reproduce known z-scores+dummies, cross-sectional regression recovers exact factor returns (zero-residual fit), statistical model reconstructs the covariance (full-rank `Total²=vᵀSv`), **factor+specific=total**, **component contributions sum to total** + factor+specific contributions sum to total, tracking-error zero-vs-self, **factor-VaR reconciles with MODEL-01e Monte-Carlo VaR within 10%** (same history, seeded), ShockReturn/VaR exactness, blend factor-count + block-diagonal cov, scenario reval moves exposure + catalog dispatch. **Verified**: `go build ./...`, `go vet ./internal/risk/...`, `gofmt` clean; `go test ./internal/risk/...` green; arch test = only the 3 pre-existing failures. No `-race` locally (no cgo on the dev box); CI runs it. **Carried-forward** (seams delivered): the live `CharacteristicProvider` from a reference store + the `ModelProvider` rebuilt nightly at the engine composition root (static providers + the wire schema today, the same MODEL-01 integration the FI/Greek providers await); a Bayesian/Ledoit-Wolf shrinkage estimator for the factor covariance (sample cov + a tiny ridge today); GARCH/EWMA factor-vol weighting (equal-weight sample now); and exact-date return alignment (tail-alignment today, the MODEL-01b refinement). **Next in Phase-6**: XVA-01 (counterparty/valuation adjustments — the priced DERIV/FI instruments feed it), then COLL/STRUCT/REG/IBOR/POST.

**Phase-5 LIQ-01a–e DONE — LIQ-01 epic COMPLETE** (liquidity risk — liquidation-horizon, Almgren-Chriss-style market impact, and liquidity-adjusted VaR; the liquidity dimension the `market.v1` quote feed already carries (spread + top-of-book depth) but nothing modelled, ROI #30). All inside `kanz/internal/risk/` (no RISK-02 boundary edge — arch test still shows only the 3 pre-existing failures: `lake/dataset`, `oms/position`, `contract/tenancy`). **LIQ-01a** (`kanz-schemas/proto/reference/v1/liquidity.proto`): additive to `reference.v1` — `LiquidityProfile` (`instrument_id`+`as_of` point-in-time key like BondTerms/YieldCurve/InstrumentReference; `average_daily_volume` Decimal in natural units, `average_spread` Decimal as a fraction of price, optional per-instrument `participation_rate`, advisory `currency_code`). The smoothed liquidity REFERENCE the models calibrate to; a live `market.v1.Quote`'s instantaneous spread/depth is the realized observation. SDK stays generated-not-committed (EVT-15a — gen/go gitignored, CI regenerates via real buf; the internal package, like FI-01's BondSpec, carries its own float working shape so the kanz build needs no regen). **LIQ-01b** (`internal/risk/liquidity/horizon.go`): `LiquiditySpec` (the float shape behind LiquidityProfile) + `Provider` seam (the FI BondTermsProvider mirror, resolved at `p.AsOf()`); `Model{ParticipationRate, ImpactCoeff}` (DefaultModel = 20% participation, impact 1.0); `DaysToLiquidate = |qty|/(participation·ADV)` (monotonic ↑ in size, ↓ in ADV; **ADV≤0 ⇒ ok=false + +Inf** — not-liquidatable, flagged not finite); `LiquidationProfile` walks base-currency positions into per-name `PositionHorizon`s + `MaxDays` (slowest liquid name gates a parallel unwind) / notional-weighted `WeightedDays` / `IlliquidNotional`. **LIQ-01c** (`impact.go` + `lvar.go`): `CostFraction = ½·spread·(1+impactCoeff·(√days−1))` — half-spread to cross, widened by √horizon (Almgren-Chriss temporary impact; horizon already encodes size/ADV), `=½·spread` at a 1-day horizon, **capped at 1** (can't lose >100%, which also bounds an +Inf-day illiquid name at its notional rather than blowing up — the ADV-zero handling); `LiquidationCost = Σ|MV_i|·CostFraction_i` (base-currency only); `LiquidityAdjustedVaR = VaR + LiquidationCost` (Bangia-style exogenous-spread add-on widened by the horizon — **≥ VaR by construction**, cost is never negative). **LIQ-01d** (`compute/liquidity.go` + `liquidity/stress.go` + `scenario/library/liquidity.go`): `RegisterLiquidityRisk(ctx,r,provider,model,baseVaR)` registers `LiquidationHorizon` (notional-weighted days; illiquid names surfaced via the Profile, kept out of the scalar since +Inf has no Decimal) + `LVaR99` (closes over the already-registered VaR measure — placeholder OR MODEL-01d historical, nil ⇒ VaR99 — so LVaR tracks whichever VaR the engine serves, money scale = cents); the FI-01d/DERIV-01d enrichment-seam pattern. A liquidity stress is NOT a price shock — `liquidity.Stress{SpreadMult, ADVMult}.Wrap(provider)` transforms the Provider (spreads ×, ADV ÷), so every liquidity measure recomputes under stress with no other change (EvaluateReval blast-radius discipline, one axis over); the library catalog (`LIQUIDITY_STRESS` ×3 spread/÷3 ADV, `LIQUIDITY_CRISIS` ×5/÷5) is the liquidity-axis counterpart of the GICS price curves + rate curves, `NamedLiquidityStress` the dispatch seam. **LIQ-01e** (`liquidity/liquidity_test.go`, `compute/liquidity_test.go`, `scenario/library/liquidity_test.go`): horizon monotonic in size (100k→0.5d, 1M→5d) + ADV-zero (ok=false/+Inf), CostFraction (1-day=half-spread, 4-day=½·spread·√4, cap-at-1 for +Inf), LiquidationProfile aggregation (MaxDays=5, WeightedDays=2.0, IlliquidNotional, no-data skipped), **stress widens horizon AND cost**, **LVaR ≥ VaR** (both the pure `LiquidityAdjustedVaR` and the registered `LVaR99` ≥ `VaR99`), registered-horizon = weighted days, stress raises both registered measures, named-catalog resolution. **Verified**: `go build ./...`, `go vet ./internal/risk/...`, `gofmt` clean; `go test ./internal/risk/...` green; arch test = only the 3 pre-existing failures. No `-race` locally (no cgo on the dev box); CI runs it. **Carried-forward** (seams delivered): a reference store feeding the live `liquidity.Provider` from `LiquidityProfile` (in-memory provider + the wire schema today — the same engine-composition-root wiring the FI `CurveProvider` / Greek providers await); endogenous market-impact calibrated from the volatility model (σ·√participation) rather than the spread·√horizon proxy; intraday/decaying participation profiles (constant-rate today); and an FX layer for cross-currency liquidation cost (base-currency-only now, the RISK-07 convention). **This completes the Phase-5 PM/quant analytics arc** (DERIV-01, FI-01, PERF-01, OPT-01, LIQ-01); next is the Phase-6 "Institutional Risk & Capital Depth" roadmap (FACTOR/XVA/COLL/STRUCT/REG/IBOR/POST).

**Phase-5 OPT-01a–f DONE — OPT-01 epic COMPLETE** (portfolio construction & optimization — the PM's forward workflow: construct under the SAME COMP-01 mandate constraints, propose human-in-the-loop trades, materialize OMS-01 orders, ROI #29). New `kanz/internal/optimization/` (pure solver) + `kanz/services/optimization/` (the construction service + the proposal→OMS bridge). **Outside the risk module ⇒ covariance is an INPUT, not a reach-in (RISK-02)**: optimization lives outside `internal/risk`, so it cannot import the MODEL-01e covariance estimator — it takes Σ + μ as `MarketInputs` and ships its own `SampleCovariance` (the performance/COMP-01 own-the-concept precedent). It DOES reuse `internal/compliance` directly (no boundary there) — the "one source of truth" the task demands; arch test shows only the 3 pre-existing failures. **OPT-01a** (`kanz-schemas/proto/optimization/v1/optimization.proto`, CODEOWNERS `@kanz-eng/risk-engine @kanz-eng/quant-ml @kanz-eng/execution`): `ObjectiveType`/`ObjectiveSpec` (max-return/min-variance/max-Sharpe/risk-parity + risk-aversion/risk-free), `ConstraintSet` (`WeightBound`s, `SectorCap`s, turnover, long-only), `TargetWeight`, `ProposedTrade` (+`TradeSide`), `RebalanceProposal` (targets + trades + expected return/risk/turnover + `mandate_feasible`/violations) — weights/returns are `double` per EVT-14. gen/go regenerated via the protocompile driver (buf blocked); SDK generated-not-committed (EVT-15a). **OPT-01b** (`optimizer.go` + `solver.go`): a small, deterministic, **dependency-free** solver (no gonum/QP lib) — `projectBudgetBox` (Euclidean projection onto {lo≤w≤hi, Σw=1} by water-filling on one budget multiplier) is the workhorse; MinVariance + mean-variance-blend via projected-gradient descent (Gershgorin step), MaxReturn via projecting a large multiple of μ (the LP vertex), MaxSharpe via the tangency closed form (`Σ⁻¹(μ−rf)` by in-module Gaussian elimination) + projected refinement, **RiskParity via the DAMPED multiplicative fixed point `wᵢ←wᵢ·√(bᵢ/rcᵢ)`** (the un-damped `1/(Σw)ᵢ` oscillates — the square-root damping is what converges to equal risk contributions); `SampleCovariance` + `RiskContributions` helpers. **OPT-01c** (`constraints.go`): the feasible region (box bounds from long-only/global/per-instrument) PLUS the COMP-01 reuse — `MandateConstraints` translates a mandate's INSTRUMENT-concentration→max-weight, DENY-restriction→0-weight, SECTOR-concentration→sector-cap into solver constraints, and `CheckMandate` projects the target weights into a `compliance.Book` and runs the **exact** COMP-01 engine the pre-trade gate uses (deny-by-default) so rules a box can't express (leverage/currency/issuer aggregation) are still enforced — a book you can't hold, you can't optimize into. **OPT-01d** (`rebalance.go`): `Rebalance` diffs current vs target into a MINIMAL trade list (|Δw|≤threshold ⇒ no dust trade), computes side/quantity (notional/price)/turnover (Σ|Δw|/2); `Propose` is the full pipeline (optimize→CheckMandate→rebalance), returning an infeasible proposal FLAGGED (`MandateFeasible=false`+violations) not silently dropped — the PM sees why. **OPT-01e** (`services/optimization/internal/bridge/` + service): `ToOrders` maps an APPROVED, feasible proposal→`order.v1.SubmitOrder` commands (market/DAY, **issuer-bound on `CommandMetadata.issuer`** — AUTH-01c, deterministic order_id for EVT-17d idempotency); `Materialize` re-checks each against the COMP-01 pre-trade `Gate` (deny-by-default — a drifted target caught before it's a live order) and publishes admitted ones via a `Publisher` seam (DEBT-02 inject-the-side-effect); a stateless HTTP service (`POST /v1/propose|orders` + probes/metrics, house style, boots ready). **OPT-01f** (`*_test.go` across the package + bridge/server): known MinVariance frontier point (1/var² → 0.8/0.2), MaxSharpe tangency (Σ⁻¹μ → 0.667/0.333), **RiskParity equal-risk-contribution** (RC_A=RC_B), MaxReturn caps bind (B capped at 0.6, spill to A), MinVariance max-weight binds, SampleCovariance (anti-correlated⇒negative), input validation; rebalance minimality (dust skipped, turnover, side/quantity), Propose mandate-infeasible-flagged + feasible-passes + MandateConstraints box derivation; bridge proposal→order mapping (issuer bound, sides, qty, infeasible⇒no orders) + Materialize gate-rejects-not-published + publish-error-propagates; the 3 HTTP endpoints. **Verified**: `go build ./...`, `go vet`, `gofmt` clean; `go test ./internal/optimization/... ./services/optimization/...` green; gen/go + kanz build against the regenerated `optimization.pb.go`; arch test = only the 3 pre-existing failures. No `-race` locally (no cgo on the dev box); CI runs it. **Carried-forward** (seams delivered): the concrete bus `Publisher` + the live COMP-01 `Gate`/`MandateRegistry` wired at the composition root (the service serves optimize/propose + dry-run-materialize today; the same OMS/reference integration the prior epics await); transaction-cost-aware turnover penalties in the objective (turnover is reported + capped, not yet penalized in the solve); and feeding the live MODEL-01e covariance/expected-returns into `MarketInputs` (request-supplied today). **Next in Phase-5**: LIQ-01 (liquidity risk), then the remaining Phase-5 epics.

**Phase-5 PERF-01a–f DONE — PERF-01 epic COMPLETE** (performance measurement & attribution — the "how did we do and why" the LAKE-01 backtest plane + MODEL-01b bitemporal store were built to support, ROI #28). New top-level **`kanz/internal/performance/`** (pure compute) + **`kanz/services/performance/`** (the reporting service). **Outside the risk module ⇒ own seams (RISK-02)**: performance lives outside `internal/risk`, so the arch boundary forbids importing the risk impl packages — like COMP-01 it carries its OWN `Classifier` (sector bucketing) + position/flow provider seams rather than reaching into `factor`/`compute`; the price history it reads point-in-time IS the shared `internal/marketdata/store` (not risk-internal), so no boundary is crossed (arch test shows only the 3 pre-existing failures). **PERF-01a** (`kanz-schemas/proto/performance/v1/performance.proto`, CODEOWNERS `@kanz-eng/risk-engine @kanz-eng/quant-ml`): `ReturnPeriod` (TWR/MWR + `as_of` knowledge horizon), `BenchmarkDefinition` (weighted `BenchmarkConstituent`s with sector), `SectorAttribution`/`AttributionResult` (allocation/selection/interaction + reconciling `active_return`), `RiskStatistics` (TE/IR/Sharpe/beta) — every return/weight/effect is `double` per the EVT-14 statistic-vs-money rule. gen/go regenerated locally via the protocompile driver (buf blocked); SDK generated-not-committed (EVT-15a). **PERF-01b** (`returns.go` + `valuation.go`): `SubPeriod`/`TimeWeightedReturn` (geometric linking, flow-neutral), `Period.ModifiedDietz` + `Period.IRR` (true money-weighted, bisection with Dietz fallback), `Annualize`; the `Valuer` marks holdings off the store's bitemporal point-in-time price (`History` with `End`=observation horizon, `AsOf`=knowledge horizon) — **the no-future-leakage hinge**: a restatement stamped after `asOf` is invisible (MODEL-01i). **PERF-01c** (`benchmark.go` + `services/performance/`): `Benchmark.Return` (weight-renormalized Σ wᵢrᵢ) + `ActiveReturn` + `InstrumentReturn` (point-in-time price return, one price basis with the valuation reads); the service is a **stateless analytics HTTP endpoint** (`POST /v1/returns|attribution|risk` + probes/metrics, house service style — config/server/observability, boots ready, no broker/DB). **PERF-01d** (`attribution.go`): Brinson-Hood-Beebower `Brinson` (allocation=(wp−wb)Rb, selection=wb(Rp−Rb), interaction=(wp−wb)(Rp−Rb) — sum exactly to Rp−Rb), `LinkCarino` multi-period linking (each period's arithmetic effects scaled by kₜ/k so they sum to the GEOMETRICALLY-linked active return — the Carino identity Σ kₜ(Rpₜ−Rbₜ)=ln(gp/gb)), and `BucketBySector` (own `Classifier`, `UNCLASSIFIED` catch-all so the decomposition reconciles — the factor.SectorExposure reconciliation invariant). **PERF-01e** (`expost.go`): `ExPostRisk` — tracking error (annualized σ of active returns), information ratio, Sharpe, benchmark beta (cov/var), Bessel-corrected sample stats, zero-denominator-safe. **PERF-01f** (`*_test.go` across the package + `server_test.go`): TWR flow-neutrality (flow doesn't distort the 21% link), Modified-Dietz known vector (26/125=0.208), IRR no-flow (10% over a 731-day leap span), Annualize, **point-in-time no-future-leakage** (a day-20 restatement invisible to an as-of-day-10 read, visible at day-21 — over the real store), Brinson-effects-sum-to-active, Carino multi-period→geometric active, BucketBySector aggregation+unclassified, ex-post beta=2/TE=0-when-identical/Sharpe-sign/insufficient-data, and the 3 HTTP endpoints (incl. bad-body 400). **Verified**: `go build ./...`, `go vet`, `gofmt` clean; `go test ./internal/performance/... ./services/performance/...` green; gen/go + kanz build against the regenerated `performance.pb.go`. No `-race` locally (no cgo on the dev box); CI runs it. **Carried-forward** (seams delivered): auto-valuing a portfolio from its live position history (the service computes over request-supplied valuations/inputs today; the `PositionProvider`/`FlowProvider` seams await the same reference/position-store integration the risk engine + FI/Greek providers wait on), and a holdings-based benchmark return from constituent point-in-time prices wired into the service (the `Benchmark`/`InstrumentReturn` primitives are delivered). **Next in Phase-5**: OPT-01 (portfolio construction & optimization), then LIQ-01.

**Phase-5 FI-01a–f DONE — FI-01 epic COMPLETE** (fixed-income analytics — yield-curve construction + bond rate risk, completing the MODEL-01 curve-shift deferral ROI #27 and giving the `FIXED_INCOME` asset class real measures). All inside `kanz/internal/risk/` (no RISK-02 boundary edge — arch test still shows only the 3 pre-existing failures: `lake/dataset`, `oms/position`, `contract/tenancy`). **FI-01a** (`kanz-schemas/proto/reference/v1/fixed_income.proto`): additive to `reference.v1` — `BondTerms` (face/coupon/`PaymentFrequency` reused from contract.proto via import/issue+maturity/`DayCountConvention` enum/currency/issuer_id) + `YieldCurve` (`curve_id`+`as_of` point-in-time key, `CurveType` ZERO/PAR/DISCOUNT, `Compounding`, `Interpolation`, repeated `CurvePoint{tenor_years double, rate Decimal}` — tenor is a time measure so `double` is correct, rate stays `Decimal`). gen/go regenerated locally via the pure-Go **protocompile** driver (buf.exe still Application-Control-blocked — see memory; protocompile@v0.14.1 from the module cache parses the .proto, the driver injects managed-mode `go_package` and shells `protoc-gen-go.exe`), `fixed_income.pb.go` shares `package referencev1`; SDK stays generated-not-committed (EVT-15a). **FI-01b** (`pricing/curve/`): `Curve` stores continuously-compounded zeros on ascending pillars (`Zero`/`Discount`/`Forward`, linear-zero + log-linear-DF interpolation, flat extrapolation), `NewZeroCurve` converts annual/semi/continuous inputs; `Bootstrap` is the textbook sequential par-instrument bootstrap (`DF_n = (1 − c_n·Σ τ_i DF_i)/(1 + c_n τ_n)`) with `ParRate` the exact inverse (round-trips); `*Curve` is the real impl behind the DERIV-01b `pricing.DiscountCurve` seam (FlatCurve was the placeholder). **FI-01c** (`pricing/bond.go` + `daycount.go`): `DayCount` (ACT/ACT-ISDA, ACT/365F, ACT/360, 30/360) `YearFraction`; `Bond` (bullet/zero-coupon) — `PriceFromYield` (periodic compounding at coupon freq), `YieldFromPrice` (bisection), `AccruedInterest`/`CleanPrice`, `Risk` (one-pass Macaulay/modified duration + convexity + DV01 from analytic dP/dy, d²P/dy²), and curve-based `PriceFromCurve`/`EffectiveDuration`/`CurveRisk`/`KeyRateDurations` (single-pillar bumps tile the curve ⇒ ΣKRD ≈ effective duration). **FI-01d** (`compute/fi.go`): `RegisterFIRisk` registers DV01/Duration/Convexity/SpreadDuration on the RISK-07 registry, the bond mirror of DERIV-01d Greeks — measures close over `FIProviders{BondTermsProvider, CurveProvider}` resolved per-position at `p.AsOf()`; DV01 = Σ dv01·qty (dollar), Duration/Convexity/SpreadDuration are |MV|-weighted (SpreadDuration ≡ Duration for a bullet — the credit-spread seam STRUCT-01d refines); non-bond positions contribute nothing. **FI-01e** (`compute/bondreval.go` + `scenario/curvereval.go` + `scenario/library/curve.go`): `curve.Shift` primitives (`Parallel`/`Steepener`/`Butterfly`, bp-denominated, pure-clone), `compute.BondRevaluer` reprices a bond position MV×(P_shifted/P_base) off the shifted curve, and `scenario.EvaluateCurveShift` is the bond sibling of DERIV-01e `EvaluateReval` (separate entry point, zero blast radius; non-bonds untouched by a rate move) — lands the MODEL-01 curve-shift carried-forward; the library `NamedCurveShift` catalog (RATES_UP/DOWN_100, BEAR_STEEPENER, BULL_FLATTENER, BUTTERFLY_25) is the rate-world counterpart of the GICS equity scenarios. **FI-01f** (`curve/*_test.go`, `pricing/bond_test.go`+`daycount_test.go`, `compute/fi_test.go`, `scenario/curvereval_test.go`): flat-curve DF/rate + interpolation + compounding-conversion + log-linear-constant-forward, bootstrap round-trip + descending DFs, par-bond price-to-par + known Macaulay 4.1699/modified 3.7908/convexity 19.37 vectors, DV01-vs-bump, yield round-trip, accrued/clean (30/360 half-period = 5.0), zero-coupon (MacDur≈maturity), ΣKRD≈effective duration, day-count matrix (ACT/360, ACT/365F, 30/360 EOM, ACT/ACT leap), portfolio FI-measure aggregation + non-bond-is-zero, curve-shift repricing (rates-up lowers bond MV, rates-down raises, equity untouched), catalog resolution. **Verified**: `go build ./...`, `go vet ./internal/risk/...`, `gofmt` clean; `go test ./internal/risk/...` green; gen/go + kanz both build against the regenerated `fixed_income.pb.go`. No `-race` locally (no cgo on the dev box); CI runs it. **Carried-forward** (seams delivered): a reference store feeding the live `BondTermsProvider`/`CurveProvider` (in-memory providers + the `YieldCurve`/`BondTerms` wire schemas today; the engine-composition-root wiring is the same open MODEL-01 integration seam the Greek providers wait on); a `ContractTerms`-style oneof carrying `BondTerms` for point-in-time joins; true OAS/effective-duration-under-optionality (STRUCT-01d, where SpreadDuration diverges from Duration); and exact-date (vs consecutive-difference) coupon schedules in the bootstrap. **Next in Phase-5**: PERF-01 (performance measurement & attribution), then OPT-01 / LIQ-01.

**Phase-5 DERIV-01a–f DONE — DERIV-01 epic COMPLETE** (derivatives pricing & Greeks — turns the ASSET_CLASS_DERIVATIVE tag into analytics, replacing the RISK-07 placeholder Delta with real per-instrument option pricing). All inside `kanz/internal/risk/` (so no RISK-02 boundary concern). **DERIV-01a** (`kanz-schemas/proto/reference/v1/contract.proto`): `OptionTerms` (underlying/strike/expiry/type/exercise/multiplier), `SwapTerms` (pay/receive `SwapLeg`s), `FutureTerms`, wrapped in a `ContractTerms` oneof keyed by instrument_id with `as_of` for point-in-time reads — additive to reference.v1. gen/go regenerated with the pure-Go protocompile driver, now setting the buf `;<pkg>` Go-package-name suffix so `contract.pb.go` shares `package referencev1` with the committed `instrument.pb.go` (the driver fix also re-emitted compliance/v1 as `compliancev1` for consistency). **DERIV-01b** (`kanz/internal/risk/pricing/`): closed-form Black-Scholes-Merton (with dividend yield q) + analytic Greeks, and a Cox-Ross-Rubinstein binomial tree for American options + model-agnostic bumped Greeks (1%-of-spot bump to clear CRR node-spacing noise on Gamma). Float64 internals, Decimal at the measure edge (the compute.volatility/VaR stance). Every Greek is the partial w.r.t. its own variable (Theta is ∂V/∂time-to-expiry; calendar decay is −Theta) so each validates against a central finite difference with no sign bookkeeping. A `DiscountCurve` seam (FlatCurve default) is where FI-01's real curve plugs in. **DERIV-01c** (`pricing/volsurface/`): a bisection implied-vol solve (robust where Newton's vega collapses), a strike×expiry `Surface` with bilinear interpolation + edge-clamp (flat extrapolation — never invents curvature), built from market option quotes; a point-in-time `QuoteProvider` seam (the MODEL-01b mirror). **DERIV-01d** (`compute/greeks.go`): `RegisterGreeks` registers Delta/Gamma/Vega/Theta/Rho on the RISK-07 registry, **overwriting the placeholder Delta**, each a MeasureFunc closing over four providers (Terms/Spot/Vol/Curve — the reference mirror of the vol-model enrichment) resolved per-position point-in-time at `p.AsOf()`. Dollar (cash) Greeks: Delta=Σ Δ·S·n, Gamma=Σ Γ·S²·n, Vega/Theta/Rho=Σ greek·n (n=qty×multiplier); a non-option position is linear (Δ≡1 ⇒ contributes its net MarketValue), so a cash-equity book's Delta equals its net exposure exactly as the placeholder did. **DERIV-01e** (`compute/reval.go` + `scenario/reval.go` + api/v1 `VolShock`): `EvaluateReval` is the sibling of `Evaluate` that full-reval-reprices option positions through BS/binomial under the scenario's price+vol shocks (capturing gamma/vega), scaling non-option positions linearly — a separate entry point, zero blast radius on the existing linear path; plus `pricing.DeltaGammaVaR` (the second-order parametric VaR variant, evaluating both move directions so a short-gamma book's asymmetric downside shows) and a `library.VolSpikeRiskOff` builder (−20% + +15 vol points; kept out of the GICS-curve `Named` catalog by design). **DERIV-01f** (`pricing/*_test.go` + `compute/greeks_test.go`,`reval_test.go`): BS reference values (Hull call 10.4506 / put 5.5735), put-call parity, analytic Greeks vs finite-difference (all five), binomial→BS convergence + American-put early-exercise premium + binomial-vs-analytic Greeks, degenerate (expired) inputs, delta-gamma VaR (long-gamma reduces downside below delta-only), implied-vol round-trip + arbitrage-band rejection, surface round-trip/interpolation/clamp + incomplete-grid error, portfolio-Greek aggregation (net Delta/Gamma across long+short options + equity fallback), and option full-reval (nonlinear price reprice + vega). **Verified**: `go build ./...`, `go vet`, `gofmt` clean; `go test ./internal/risk/...` green; arch test shows DERIV adds no new boundary edge (the 3 failures remain pre-existing). **Carried-forward**: FI-01's real discount curve behind `DiscountCurve` (flat default today); SwapTerms/FutureTerms pricing (terms shipped, pricers are option-first — swaps/futures are FI-01/follow-up); wiring the Greek providers from the live reference/market stores at the engine composition root (in-memory providers + the volsurface today). **Next in Phase-5**: FI-01 (fixed-income analytics — the curve DERIV discounts off), then PERF-01 / OPT-01 / LIQ-01.

**Phase-5 COMP-01a–g DONE — COMP-01 epic COMPLETE** (compliance & mandate engine — pre-/post-trade mandate enforcement, deny-by-default, every decision audited). New `kanz/internal/compliance/` (pure engine) + `kanz/services/compliance/` (the monitor service). **COMP-01a** (`kanz-schemas/proto/compliance/v1/`): `compliance.proto` — `Mandate` (tenant/portfolio-scoped, versioned, `effective_at`), `Rule` (a `oneof` over `ConcentrationLimit`/`RestrictionList`/`IssuerExclusion`/`LeverageCap`/`CurrencyRestriction`, with `on_violation` severity), `ComplianceResult` (PASS/WARN/BREACH + `Violation`s carrying structured `evidence`); `compliance_events.proto` — the FACT-grade `ComplianceBreach` (full result + `BreachTrigger`). gen/go regenerated locally via a **pure-Go protocompile driver** (buf.exe is Application-Control-blocked on the dev box — see memory; the driver compiles the .proto and shells the local `protoc-gen-go`, emulating managed-mode `go_package`); SDK stays generated-not-committed (EVT-15a). **COMP-01b** (`engine.go`/`rules.go`): a pure `RuleFunc` registry keyed by `RuleType`, mirroring the RISK-07 `MeasureFunc` registry; **deny-by-default** (unknown rule type, params/type mismatch, or unverifiable input ⇒ BREACH, never PASS); exact ratio math via `math/big.Rat` (no float for weights/leverage). **By the RISK-02 boundary**, compliance does NOT import the risk internals (`compute`/`domain` are private to the risk module — the arch test enforces it); it carries its own `Book` built from the shared `domain.v1.PositionState` wire schema and reimplements the gross-exposure fold (same `Σ|MV|` definition `ComputeExposure` uses). **COMP-01c** (`gate.go` + `services/oms/internal/compliance/comp01.go`): the `PreTradeGate` projects the post-trade book (current holdings + order delta, re-marked at order price) and rejects on BREACH (WARN admits); the OMS adapter maps `order.v1.SubmitOrder`→`OrderDelta` and the gate's verdict→the OMS-01f `Breach`, so the OMS-01f seam's real body is now wired (the OMS feeds a `MandateRegistry` from the shared mandate stream and projects onto the live position `Book` via a new `Snapshot`). **COMP-01d** (`services/compliance/internal/monitor/`): consumes `risk.position.changed`, maintains a per-portfolio book, re-evaluates each mandate, and emits a FACT-grade `ComplianceBreach` **only on the transition into breach** (no spam) — the passive, market-move-induced breach (`MARKET_MOVE` vs `POSITION_CHANGE` trigger) AUTO-01 consumes to halt/escalate. **COMP-01e** (`internal/audit/`): every decision (pre-trade pass/reject + post-trade breach) → `observation.v1.DecisionLog` on a durable observation subject, the AUTH-01d `Audited`-recorder pattern → AUDIT-01 hash chain; best-effort (an audit-sink outage never fails a trade). **COMP-01f** (`internal/mandate/` + `internal/compliance/mandate*.go`): a mandate change is a `lifecycle.v1.ConfigChanged` FACT keyed per (tenant, portfolio) carrying the protojson mandate; replaying the stream into a point-in-time `MandateRegistry` (latest version with `effective_at ≤ asOf`) makes "which mandate applied when" reconstructable; one shared `MandateConsumer` feeds both the OMS gate and the monitor. **COMP-01g** (`internal/compliance/*_test.go` + `monitor_test.go`): rule matrix (concentration instrument+sector / restriction deny+allow-only / issuer exclusion / leverage allow+breach+unverifiable / currency — each allow & breach), WARN-severity does-not-block, deny-by-default (unknown rule + params mismatch), pre-trade reject blocks / compliant admits / no-mandate admits / decision recorded, point-in-time mandate resolution + idempotent replay + codec round-trip, and the passive post-trade breach (clean→breach emits once, MARKET_MOVE classified, dedup on re-breach, escalation recorded). **Verified**: `go build ./...`, `go vet ./internal/compliance/... ./services/compliance/...` clean; `go test ./internal/compliance/... ./services/compliance/...` green; the RISK-02 arch test shows compliance introduces **no** new boundary edge (the 3 failures it reports are pre-existing on HEAD — `lake/dataset`, `oms/position`, `contract/tenancy`). **Carried-forward** (seams delivered): a real instrument `Classifier` (issuer/sector/asset-class reference join — `nil` ⇒ unclassified today); true NAV/cash for leverage (a net-MV funded-book proxy until a portfolio-equity source lands); FX for multi-currency ratios (single-currency correct now, the `CurrencyRestriction` rule guards it); a reference/market price for valuing market orders pre-trade (limit price used, else zero incremental — the existing book is still fully checked); and a broadcast (vs queue-group) subscription for the OMS mandate registry. **Next in Phase-5**: the pricing/PM epics (DERIV-01, FI-01, PERF-01, OPT-01, LIQ-01).

**Phase-5 OMS-01b–g DONE — OMS-01 epic COMPLETE** (order & execution management — the platform's first command-driven write path). New `kanz/services/oms/` + the gateway write surface. **OMS-01b** (`internal/order/`): a pure aggregate state machine (`aggregate.go` — `Accept` validates a `SubmitOrder` and returns PENDING_NEW; `ApplyFill` folds a fill with exact weighted-average-cost VWAP + an over-fill guard; `Cancel`/`Expire`/`Amend` with terminal-state guards) behind a `Store` seam (`store.go` — in-memory default; a durable/replayable Postgres backend plugs in behind `Save`/`Load` per the PERS-01 stance). The `Service` command handler (`service.go`) is the `bus.EventHandler`: per delivery it decodes the order command, runs the pre-trade gate, admits/refuses, persists, and emits the lifecycle FACTs + the universal `command.v1.CommandOutcome` — with the bus error discipline (transient store/publish faults nack/retry; a bad command is acked after a REJECTED outcome so a poison command never blocks the partition). Idempotent re-submit: one order per `order_id`. **OMS-01c** (`internal/execution/`): a `Router` (SOR seam, first-venue default) + a `Venue` seam with an in-process `SimVenue` that fills marketable orders deterministically at the limit/mark price (a real FIX adapter is composition-root). **OMS-01d** (`services/api-gateway/internal/orders/`): `POST /v1/orders` + `/v1/orders/{id}/cancel` behind the existing edge chain — binds the authenticated `middleware.Principal` as `CommandMetadata.issuer` (**overriding any client-supplied value — the issuer cannot be forged**), stamps tenant, keys envelope dedup on the `Idempotency-Key` header, and publishes a COMMAND through a producer wired with `auth.VerifyCommandIssuer` (the AUTH-01c second layer). Read-only when no `API_GATEWAY_NATS_URL` (routes 503). **OMS-01e** (`internal/position/`): a fill→position projector folding fills into a `Book` (exact WACC, realized P&L booked on reductions, lot re-opened on a zero-cross) that republishes `domain.v1.PositionState` on `risk.position.changed` — the **existing** risk-engine input, so traded positions flow into exposures/measures with no risk-engine change. **OMS-01f** (`internal/compliance/`): the pre-trade `Gate` seam (deny-by-default once wired); `AllowAll` default; COMP-01 implements the same interface and injects at the composition root. **OMS-01g**: aggregate transitions (partial→full VWAP, over-fill, cancel/amend guards), service flow (submit→route→fill→EXECUTED outcome, idempotent re-submit emits nothing new, compliance-breach rejects before accept + persists nothing, cancel-unknown → REJECTED/UNKNOWN_ORDER), sim-venue fill/rest, position WACC + zero-cross realized P&L, and the gateway issuer-binding (forged body issuer overridden, unauthenticated 401, writes-disabled 503). **Verified**: `go build ./...`, `go vet ./...`, `gofmt` all clean; `go test ./services/oms/... ./services/api-gateway/...` green. Regenerated `gen/go` (Go-only template, no network) so the `order.v1` types resolve; SDK stays generated-not-committed (EVT-15a). No `-race` locally (no cgo on the dev box); CI runs it. **Carried-forward** (seams delivered): a durable order `Store` (Postgres) + the Kafka snapshot/replay bootstrap (the in-memory store + bus dedup are the floor); a real FIX/venue adapter behind `Venue`; the COMP-01 engine behind `Gate`; and the position projector's per-instrument currency (a reference-data join, MODEL-01 carried-forward — `BaseCurrency` until then). **Next in Phase-5**: COMP-01 (the compliance engine — its `Gate` impl is the OMS-01f seam's real body), then the pricing/PM epics.

**Phase-5 OMS-01a DONE** (order/execution schemas — opens the OMS-01 epic and the platform's first first-class COMMAND flow). New `kanz-schemas/proto/order/v1/` (package `order.v1`, CODEOWNERS `@kanz-eng/execution`), split like `command.v1`: **`order.proto`** — the concrete order COMMANDS `SubmitOrder`/`AmendOrder`/`CancelOrder`, each embedding `command.v1.CommandMetadata` as field 1 per the command-class contract (`metadata.target_id` == `order_id` == envelope `partition_key`, so all commands+FACTs for one order are totally ordered) + the order enums `Side`/`OrderType`(MARKET/LIMIT/STOP/STOP_LIMIT)/`TimeInForce`(DAY/GTC/IOC/FOK/GTD); **`order_events.proto`** — the lifecycle FACTs `OrderAccepted`/`Rejected`/`Routed`/`PartiallyFilled`/`Filled`/`Cancelled`/`Expired`, each self-contained (carries `order_id` + the resulting `OrderState` snapshot so a consumer never re-derives disposition), the `OrderStatus` state machine (PENDING_NEW→ROUTED→PARTIALLY_FILLED→{FILLED|CANCELLED|REJECTED|EXPIRED}), and the atomic `Fill` (the FIX-style execution-report record: `fill_id` idempotency, qty/price, `fee` Money for realized-PnL, venue MIC + `venue_execution_id` for the POST-01 reconciliation seam). All `Decimal`/`Money`/timestamp use the `common.v1` primitives per house convention (never float). **Verified**: `buf build` resolves all imports (incl. the `command/v1` + `common/v1` deps); `buf lint` clean on `order/v1` (the two pre-existing `inference_service.proto` RPC-naming findings are untouched). SDK is generated-not-committed (EVT-15a) — `buf generate` emits the Go/Py/TS stubs downstream; nothing under `gen/` committed here. **Next in OMS-01**: 01b (the `oms` service — validate→accept→emit over a durable order aggregate), then 01c (EMS `Venue` seam), 01d (gateway write surface), 01e (fill→position), 01f (COMP-01 pre-trade gate), 01g (tests).

**Phase-4 DEVX-01a–e DONE — DEVX-01 epic COMPLETE; PHASE-4 COMPLETE** (developer productivity platform — the last Phase-4 epic). **DEVX-01b** (`tools/scaffold/`, the substantive Go deliverable): a generator that emits an on-convention service skeleton (`services/<name>/cmd/<name>/main.go` + per-service `internal/config` + `internal/server`, wired with the standard observability + probes) following the EVT-16a layout. Validates the name (lowercase DNS-label), refuses to overwrite (no half-scaffolded tree), and **gofmt's its Go output on write** (so a broken template fails generation, not the downstream build). Tested: generated files parse, env-prefix/import-path substitution lands, overwrite refused, bad names rejected — and verified out-of-band that a generated service `go build`s against the real module. **DEVX-01a** (`dev/docker-compose.yml` + `Tiltfile`): one-command local stack — NATS + Kafka (single-node KRaft) + Postgres + risk-engine + api-gateway, seeded via the existing `test/load/seed` (no duplication). `make up` / `tilt up`. **DEVX-01c** (`infra/backstage/`): the portal — `catalog-info.yaml` (the `kanz` System + a Component per service with owners/TechDocs/source links + the `risk-query-v1` API) and a `template.yaml` "New Kanz service" software template that runs the **same** DEVX-01b generator (one source of truth for the skeleton). **DEVX-01d** (`.github/workflows/preview.yml` + `infra/gitops/preview-applicationset.yaml`): ephemeral per-PR previews the GitOps-native way — Argo CD's PullRequest generator materializes one Application per `preview`-labeled PR into `kanz-preview-{number}` pinned to the PR's head_sha + `:pr-{number}` images, and **auto-destroys** it on close via `prune` (no teardown job to forget); the workflow builds/pushes those images and comments the namespace. **DEVX-01e** (`Makefile` + `docs/onboarding.md`): `make` targets (`bootstrap`, `generate`, `build`, `test`, `lint`, `up`/`down`, `scaffold NAME=…`, `ci`) that **kill the hand-run buf-generate friction the memory documented** — `GOFLAGS=-mod=mod` baked in, `bootstrap` installs protoc-gen-go-grpc, `generate` is step one (the SDK is generated-not-committed, EVT-15a); the onboarding doc is the fresh-clone-to-running path. **Verified**: `go build ./...`, `go vet`, `gofmt` clean; `go test ./tools/scaffold/...` green; a generated service compiles against the real module; all DEVX YAML parses; the Makefile is tab-indented (17 recipe lines, no space-indent). No `-race` locally (no cgo on the dev box); CI runs it. **Carried-forward**: a devcontainer.json (the compose stack is the primary path; devcontainers are an additive convenience) and live-update wiring in the Tiltfile are nice-to-haves. **Phase-4 "Scale & Strategic Bets" is now fully complete** (INFRA-01, LATENCY-01, SEC-02, DEBT-02/03, LAKE-01, LIN-01, AUTO-01, DEVX-01); only the externally-blocked DEBT-01 (SDK publishing) remains deferred across the whole board.
